Let's inspect the problem as minimizing max q_i over simplex. At local optimum, active constraints gradient combination constant on support. We can polish using a custom "exactly solve active-set linearized" method:
Given current a, sum=1. Let active set A = {i: q_i ≥ M - ε}. We want find direction d (sum=0, maybe nonnegative) that decreases M. The directional derivative of M along d (if active set remains) is max_{i∈A} 2 (rev? grad_i · d). To decrease M, need all active gradients dot d <0. This is a linear feasibility problem in d (plus maybe perturb). But d can be limited by nonnegativity. _COEFF_N = None
_COEFF_BETA = None

def coeff_objective(u):
    n=_COEFF_N; beta=_COEFF_BETA
    theta=zeros(n); theta[:-1]=u; theta[-1] = -u.sum()
    # For numerical: m=max(theta); w=exp(theta-m); w/=w.sum(); a=w
    c=fast_conv(a)
    cm=c.max(); scaled=beta*(c-cm); shifted; lse=cm+.../beta; score=2n*lse
    if not finite return 1e100
    p=exp_scaled/sum
    rev_a=a[::-1]
    M2=len(p)+n-1; fft; dL=2*irfft(...)[n-1:n-1+n]
    g_a=2*n*dL
    gtheta=g_a - a*np.sum(g_a)
    grad_u = gtheta[:-1] - gtheta[-1]
    return score, grad_u

This computes a via softmax each evaluation. For large θ, exp shift. u from L-BFGS may grow; but softmax stable. If some a very small, fine.

Exact local polishing idea:

  for iter:

Another exact approach: "Peak portal" coordinate descent. For each top peak i, solve for direction that moves mass from products causing high q_i to low. import numpy as np, time, scipy.optimize.minimize.

fast_conv(a): as above.
exact_score(a): uses fast_conv, returns float. If a list? convert to np array float.

normalize_a(a): clip 0..1000? Since objective scale invariant but cap 1000. We can return normalized sum1 and max <=1000? If a sum positive, a=a/max? For internal, set sum1, no need cap because max of normalized a may be >1000 if n small? For n large max <=1 likely unless sparse. For final output, choose scale to max<=1000 and sum>=0.01. Since score invariant, can scale. For internal softmax, a sum1, max could be up to1, cap no issue.

softmax_from_u(u): N from len(u)+1. If u contains nan/inf, ones.
  theta = np.empty(N); theta[:-1]=u; theta[-1] = -np.sum(u)
  m = np.max(theta); if not finite: return uniform
  w=np.exp(theta-m); s=w.sum(); if s<=0 return uniform; return w/s.

gradient from a to u:
  theta=np.log(a+1e-300); theta -= np.mean(theta); u=theta[:-1]; return u.
  If a zeros, log eps.

def objective_soft(u):
  N=_OBJ_N; beta=_OBJ_BETA
  a=softmax_from_u(u)
  c=fast_conv(a)
  cm=c.max()
  # if cm <=0 return (inf, grad zero)
  scaled=beta*(c-cm)
  # clip to avoid underflow? np.exp handles; but if beta*diff very negative, exp=0. If beta huge and c-cm negative but due to round c>cm small? cm max, c-cm <=0 but maybe positive 1e-16. Use scaled = np.minimum(scaled,0.0)
  e=np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0))
  s_e=e.sum()
  if s_e <=0: return (1e100, zeros)
  lse = cm + np.log(s_e)/beta
  score=2.0*N*lse  # since sum=1
  p=e/s_e
  # grad dL
  dL = 2.0 * _conv_weights_a(p, a)
  g_a = 2.0 * N * dL
  gtheta = g_a - a * np.sum(g_a)
  grad_u = gtheta[:-1] - gtheta[-1]
  # replace nonfinite
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)): grad_u = np.zeros(N-1)
  if not np.isfinite(score) or score <0: score=1e100
  return float(score), grad_u

Need _conv_weights_a(weights, a):
  n=len(a); M2=len(weights)+n-1; Mff=1 << (M2-1).bit_length(); Wfft=np.fft.rfft(weights,Mff); Arev=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],Mff); conv=np.fft.irfft(Wfft*Arev,Mff)[:M2]; out=conv[n-1:n-1+n]
  return out
Ensure if weights small, out length n. For weights length 2n-1. Good.

def optimize_softmax(a, target_seconds, beta_schedule=None, max_start_respects...):
  a = np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); S=a.sum(); if S<=0 uniform; else a=a/S.
  N=len(a); best_a=a; best_sc=exact_score(a)
  u = a_to_u(best_a)
  if beta_schedule is None: beta_schedule=[20,100,400,2000,10000,50000]
  # measure obj

Function exact_line_polish(a, target_seconds, max_iter=80, beta_w=2000):
  N=len(a); start=time.monotonic(); best=a; best_sc=exact_score(a); it=0; no_improve=0
  base=max(1e-5, 3.0/np.sqrt(N))
  while time.monotonic() < start+target_seconds and it<max_iter:
    c=fast_conv(best); M=c.max()
    if M <=0: break
    weights=_weights_from_peaks(c,beta_w)
    g=2*_conv_weights_a(weights,best)
    d=-(g - np.mean(g)); norm=...; if norm<1e-14 break
    d /= norm
    # Candidate steps: include previous_factor? We can store last_step. Initial base. We'll use a short adaptive: candidate_steps = base*np.array([0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1,3,10])
    best_local_t=0; best_local_sc=best_sc; best_candidate=None
    for step in candidate_steps:
       a_t = best + step*d
       if a_t.min() < -0.25*base: # too much negative; maybe use smaller? skip? Clipping can reveal false improvement by sum change? But normalize. We'll clip to0 if min<0 always; however large negative may make direction not comparable. Maybe if min < -base, reject.
          if a_t.min() < -base: continue
       a_t=np.clip(a_t,0,None); Sa=a_t.sum(); if Sa<1e-12 continue; a_t/=Sa; sc=exact_score(a_t); update.
    if best_local_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
       # refine: try steps = best_local_t * [0.5, 0.75,1.25,1.5] and maybe base to bracket
       for step in [best_local_t*0.5, best_local_t*0.75, best_local_t*1.25, best_local_t*1.5]: ... if improves update best
       best=best_candidate; it++; no_improve=0
    else:
       # Try with smaller beta_w (multi-peak) and with single peak maybe
       improved=False
       for bw in [200,5000]:
          if time ... break
          weights=...; d=...; evaluate with few steps [base*0.01,base*0.1,base]
          if improves: accept; improved=True; break
       if improved: it++; no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>=3: break
  return best,best_sc

"""
Search for non-negative sequence minimizing 2n max conv / sum^2.
Uses softmax/log-domain L-BFGS on log-sum-exp of convolution peaks, coarse global search, iterated local search, and exact active-weight line polish.

Implement `fast_conv` carefully:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==1:
       return np.array([a[0]*a[0]], dtype=float)
    M=2*n-1
    Mf=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, Mf)[:M]
    out[np.abs(out)<1e-15]=0.0
    return out
```
For large a, fa*fa maybe; no issue.

`exact_score`:
```
def exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-300: return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M<0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*a.size*M/(S*S))
```
But if a sum1, okay. Re-clipping each score costly? In line polish, a already clipped. But we can have exact_score_no_clip? Simpler: exact_score assumes a normalized and nonneg; no clip/copy. For final verify maybe. To save time, define:
```
def score_normalized(a):
    c=fast_conv(a); S=a.sum(); if S<=...; return 2n*max(c)/S*S
```
Rough? Use for internal. Since a sum1, no S scaling. Avoid normalization exact. We'll define `compute_score_raw(a)` assumes nonneg finite, sum>0. It computes S anyway. No clip.
```
def compute_score_raw(a):
   n=a.size; S=float(a.sum())
   if S<0.01? For internal sum may 1; if S<1e-12 return inf.
   c=fast_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
   return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```
This may produce negative c small? max nonnegative. If a zero, S=0.

def normalize_a(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.where(a>0,a,0) # no cap
   S=a.sum()
   if S<=0: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
   return a/S
```

`conv_weights_a(weights,a)`:
```
def conv_weights_a(weights,a):
   n=len(a)
   M2=len(weights)+n-1
   Mf=1 << (M2-1).bit_length()
   wf=np.fft.rfft(weights, Mf)
   af=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Mf)
   c=np.fft.irfft(wf*af, Mf)[:M2]
   return c[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Make sure if weights sum not 1; fine.

`softmax_from_u(u)`: Need if N=1? u length 0 -> return [1]. For N>1. If u has nonfinite, return uniform. To avoid large sum(u) overflow, if not finite use mean? We'll check.
```
def softmax_from_u(u):
   u=np.asarray(u,float).ravel()
   N=u.size+1
   if N==1: return np.ones(1)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
      return np.ones(N)/N
   theta=np.empty(N,float)
   theta[:-1]=u
   theta[-1] = -float(np.sum(u))
   m=float(np.max(theta))
   if not np.isfinite(m): return np.ones(N)/N
   w=np.exp(theta-m)
   s=float(np.sum(w))
   if s<=0: return np.ones(N)/N
   a=w/s
   # If one component underflows but okay. Ensure sum exactly 1:
   a /= a.sum()
   return a
```
But if u huge positive and negative, sum(u) may be huge but finite? np.exp(theta-m) stable. If u has values 1e308, sum could inf. Check finite; but sum can overflow to inf. Use np.sum with dtype? If inf, return uniform? Better: if not finite(sum_u) or abs>1e300, we need scale u. But L-BFGS unlikely such huge. Could add: sum_u = float(np.sum(u)); if not finite: return uniform.

`a_to_u(a)`:
```
def a_to_u(a):
   N=len(a)
   if N==1: return np.empty(0)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
   theta=np.log(a)
   theta-=float(np.mean(theta))
   return theta[:-1].copy()
```
If a zeros, log eps. Good.

`objective_soft`: Need handle beta<=0? use 1.
```
def objective_soft(u):
   N=_OBJ_N; beta=max(1.0,float(_OBJ_BETA)); u=u.astype(float,copy=False)
   a=softmax_from_u(u)
   c=fast_conv(a)
   cm=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm<=0:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(N-1)
   scaled=beta*(c-cm)
   scaled=np.where(scaled<0, scaled, 0.0)
   e=np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0))
   s_e=float(e.sum())
   if s_e<=0 or not np.isfinite(s_e):
       # use top peak
       p=np.zeros_like(c); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; s_e=1.0
   else:
       p=e/s_e
   lse=cm + float(np.log(s_e))/beta
   score=float(2.0*N*lse)
   dL=2.0*conv_weights_a(p,a)
   g_a=2.0*N*dL
   g_sum=float(np.sum(g_a))
   gtheta=g_a - a*g_sum
   grad_u=gtheta[:-1] - gtheta[-1]
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)):
       # fallback small random? zeros
       grad_u=np.zeros(N-1,float)
   if not np.isfinite(score) or score<0: score=1e100
   return score, grad_u
```
Potential: If p has only one peak, gradient dL may be large but finite. If N=1, grad zero.

def optimize_softmax(a, target_seconds, beta_schedule=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000.0); S=a.sum(); if S<=0 or a.size==0: ...
    a=a/S
    N=a.size
    best_a=a; best_sc=compute_score_raw(a)
    u=a_to_u(a)
    if target_seconds<=0: return best_a,best_sc
    if beta_schedule is None:
       if target_seconds < 30: beta_schedule=[500,5000,30000]
       elif target_seconds < 120: beta_schedule=[20,200,2000,20000,100000]
       else: beta_schedule=[10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,80000]
    if len(beta_schedule)==0: ...
    t0=time.monotonic()
    # measure
    _OBJ_N=N; _OBJ_BETA=1.0
    objective_soft(u); objective_soft(u)
    t_measure=time.monotonic()-t0
    t_obj=max(0.0002, t_measure/2.0*1.5)
    fracs=[0.08,0.10,0.14,0.18,0.22,0.28,0.23]
    # normalize fracs to sum <=0.9
    fs=fracs[:len] sum; for extra repeat last? We'll compute on fly.
    start_t=time.monotonic()
    for idx,beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
       if time.monotonic() >= start_t+target_seconds-5: break
       frac=fracs[idx] if idx<len(fracs) else fracs[-1]
       # Budget for this beta: (start_t+target_seconds - now)*frac? Not exactly; use target*frac maybe.
       allowed = max(5.0, target_seconds*frac - 2.0)
       # also not exceed overall remaining
       rem = start_t+target_seconds - time.monotonic()
       allowed = min(allowed, rem-5)
       if allowed <=1: break
       maxfun = int(max(200, min(20000, allowed/(t_obj*1.5))))
       maxiter = max(200, int(maxfun/2))
       _OBJ_N=N; _OBJ_BETA=float(beta)
       _STOP_TIME = min(_GLOBAL_DEADLINE? no, start_t+target_seconds-2, time.monotonic()+allowed)
       # But global _STOP_TIME used by all minimizations; set to start_t+target_seconds-2.
       _STOP_TIME = start_t + target_seconds - 2.0
       try:
          res = minimize(objective_soft, u, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={...})
       except Exception:
          res=None
       if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
          u_new=res.x
          if np.all(np.isfinite(u_new)):
             a_new=softmax_from_u(u_new)
             a_new=normalize_a(a_new)
             sc=compute_score_raw(a_new)
             if sc < best_sc:
                 best_a=a_new; best_sc=sc; u=a_to_u(a_new)
       # after each beta, maybe compute exact if no update? do update check anyway.
    # final exact from current u
    a_new=softmax_from_u(u); a_new=normalize...
    sc=...; update
    return best_a,best_sc
```

`perturb_a`:
```
def perturb_a(a, rng, scale):
   N=len(a); x=np.linspace(0,1,N)
   mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
   if mode==0: ap=a*np.exp(scale*rng.standard_normal(N))
   elif mode==1: freq=1+5*rng.random(); phase=2πrng.random(); ap=a*(1+scale*rng.random()*np.cos(2π freq x+phase))
   elif mode==2: blocks N//10; for b: ap block *= (1+scale*(rng.random()-0.5)*4)
   elif mode==3: # reduce peak contributors: c=fast_conv(a), k=max; top local...
   else: ap=a*np.exp(scale*np.cumsum(rng.standard_normal(N))-...)
   ap=np.where(ap>0,ap,0); S=sum; if S<=0 uniform; else ap/S; return
```
No nested. Top-level loop okay.

`exact_line_polish`: Need helper `_weights` top-level maybe.
```
def peak_weights(c, beta_w):
   M=float(c.max())
   if M<=0: return np.ones_like(c)/(len(c))
   scaled=beta_w*(c/M -1.0)
   scaled=np.where(scaled<0.0, scaled,0.0)
   e=np.exp(np.clip(scaled,-50.0,0.0)); s=e.sum()
   if s<=0:
      w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return w
   return e/s
```

`exact_line_polish` details:
```
def exact_line_polish(a, target_seconds, beta_w_list=None):
    if target_seconds<=0: return a, compute_score_raw(a)
    start=time.monotonic()
    best=np.array(a,float); best_sc=compute_score_raw(best)
    N=best.size
    base=max(1e-5, 4.0/np.sqrt(N))
    if beta_w_list is None: beta_w_list=[500,2000,10000,200]
    no_imp=0; it=0; max_it=200
    while time.monotonic() < start+target_seconds and it<max_it and no_imp<4:
       c=fast_conv(best); M=c.max()
       if M<=0: break
       for beta_w in beta_w_list:
          if time.monotonic() >= start+target_seconds: break
          w=peak_weights(c,beta_w)
          g=2.0*conv_weights_a(w,best)
          d=-(g-np.mean(g)); norm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
          if norm<1e-14: continue
          d=d/norm
          found=False
          # steps
          steps=[base*s for s in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0)]
          local_best_sc=best_sc; local_t=0.0; local_a=None
          for step in steps:
             if time.monotonic() >= start+target_seconds: break
             ap=best+step*d
             if ap.min() < -base: continue
             ap=np.clip(ap,0.0,None)
             Sa=ap.sum()
             if Sa<1e-12: continue
             ap=ap/Sa
             sc=compute_score_raw(ap)
             if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-15:
                local_best_sc=sc; local_t=step; local_a=ap; found=True
          if found:
             # refine
             factors=[0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5]
             ref_best_sc=local_best_sc; ref_a=local_a
             for fac in factors:
                 step=local_t*fac
                 ap=best+step*d; if min<-base continue; clip normalize; sc; if sc<ref_best_sc: update
             best=ref_a; best_sc=ref_best_sc; it+=1; no_imp=0; break
          # if no found for this beta_w, continue
       else:
          no_imp +=1
    return best,best_sc
```
After updating within inner beta, break to recompute c. Good. Note `for beta_w ... else` executes if no break; need manage with flag. We can use a local variable `improved=False`, if found set True and break; after loop if not improved no_imp++.

Potential cost: Recomputing c for each beta? We can compute c once per outer iteration; then for beta_w weights. If improved, break outer, next outer c updated. If no improve, no_imp. Good.

Now, final propose_candidate scheduling. Let's define:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=2400,**kwargs):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, float(budget_s)) - 20.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if p is not None:
          pnp=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
          if pnp.size>=16:
             parent=np.clip(pnp,0,1000.0)
    except: pass
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    if parent is not None:
       best_a=normalize_a(parent); best_sc=compute_score_raw(best_a); print
    else:
       a0=np.ones(1024)*0.5; best_a=a0; best_sc=compute_score_raw(a0)
    # update helper function? We'll manually.
    total_avail = max(60.0, deadline-t_start)
    if parent is not None:
        T_coarse=0.10*total_avail
    else:
        T_coarse=0.20*total_avail
    coarse_candidates=[]
    if T_coarse>30 and time.monotonic()<deadline-30:
       coarse_candidates = coarse_search(parent, rng, min(T_coarse, deadline-time.monotonic()-30))
    # Build high length list
    if parent is not None:
       n_parent=len(parent)
       n_list=[n_parent]
       n_large = int(round(n_parent*1.5))
       # maybe round to multiple 8
       if n_large < n_parent+512: n_large=n_parent+1024
       if n_large <= 20000: n_list.append(n_large)
       # maybe third even larger if time and parent small
       if n_parent < 8000 and len(n_list)==2:
          n_x = int(round(n_large*1.4))
          if n_x <= 24000: n_list.append(n_x)
    else:
       n_list=[8192,16384]
    # time fractions: dynamic, prefer parent/coarse best lengths
    # For 2: [0.55,0.45], 3: [0.45,0.35,0.20]
    ...
    for each n with time_budget:
       if deadline passed break
       # starts:
       starts=[]
       # parent interpolated
       if parent is not None:
          starts.append(interpolate_a(parent,n))
       # coarse candidates if not empty:
       for ca in coarse_candidates:
          if ca is not None: starts.append(interpolate_a(ca,n))
       # analytic:
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       for B in [-0.75,0.5,1.25]: starts.append(np.maximum(1.0+B*x,1e-6))
       starts.append(0.2+0.8*x*x) # increasing maybe
       starts.append(0.2+0.8*(1-x)*(1-x))? etc.
       # random
       starts.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,n)))
       # Limit unique non-duplicate: sort by score
       scored=[]
       for sa in starts:
          sa=normalize_a(sa)
          if sa.size != n: continue

def coarse_search(parent, rng, target_seconds):
    if target_seconds <= 5:
        return []
    start_t=time.monotonic()
    n_list=[]
    if target_seconds > 240:
        n_list=[128,256,512]
    elif target_seconds > 120:
        n_list=[128,256]
    else:
        n_list=[96,160]
    # allocate
    weights = np.array([float(n)**1.2 for n in n_list])
    fractions = weights/weights.sum()
    best1=None; sc1=np.inf; best2=None; sc2=np.inf
    for idx,n in enumerate(n_list):
        t_alloc = (target_seconds * fractions[idx]) - 5
        if t_alloc < 20: continue
        x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
        raw=[]
        if parent is not None:
            raw.append(interpolate_a(parent,n))
        raw.append(np.ones(n))
        raw.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.7,n)))
        for B in (-0.75,0.5,1.25):
            raw.append(np.maximum(1.0+B*x,1e-6))
        raw.append(0.2+0.8*x**2)
        # interpolate coarse candidate from earlier n? Not.
        # sort raw by score and take 4
        scs=[]; arrs=[]
        for r in raw:
           r=normalize_a(r); sc=compute_score_raw(r); scs.append(sc); arrs.append(r)
        order=np.argsort(np.array(scs))
        top=[]
        for i in order:
           if len(top)>=min(4,len(arrs)): break
           top.append(arrs[i])
        local_best=None; local_sc=np.inf
        per_t = t_alloc / max(1,len(top))
        for a0 in top:
           if time.monotonic() > start_t+target_seconds-10: break
           t_s = min(60.0, per_t*0.6)
           if t_s <=3: continue
           a_opt,sc_opt = optimize_softmax(a0, t_s, beta_schedule=[10,100,1000,10000])
           if sc_opt < local_sc:
               local_sc=sc_opt; local_best=a_opt
           sc_init=compute_score_raw(a0)
           if sc_init < local_sc:
               local_sc=sc_init; local_best=np.array(a0,float)
        if local_best is not None:
           elapsed = time.monotonic()-start_t
           rem = (target_seconds - elapsed) * 0.4
           rem = max(0.0, min(180.0, rem))
           if rem > 10 and time.monotonic() < start_t+target_seconds-5:
               a_opt,sc_opt = optimize_softmax(local_best, rem, beta_schedule=[200,2000,20000,100000])
               if sc_opt < local_sc: local_sc=sc_opt; local_best=a_opt
           if local_sc < sc1:
               best2,sc2,best1,sc1 = best1,sc1,local_best,local_sc
           elif local_sc < sc2:
               best2,sc2 = local_best,local_sc
        print(f"coarse n={n} best={local_sc if local_best is not None else np.inf}")
    out=[]
    if best1 is not None: out.append(best1)
    if best2 is not None: out.append(best2)
    return out
```

_STOP_TIME = 0.0
_OBJ_N = 1
_OBJ_BETA = 1.0
_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 1e100

fast_conv:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], float)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0]*a[0]], dtype=float)
    M = 2*n - 1
    Mf = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, Mf)[:M]
    out = np.where(out > 0, out, 0.0)  # suppress negative tiny? But if negative due to numerical? max okay. Maybe don't clip all to zero if truly negative impossible. Clipping small okay.
    return out
```
Clipping negative to 0 might affect max? max positive. Fine. But if all values negative due to bug? no. Could use `out[np.abs(out)<1e-14*max(1,np.max(np.abs(out)))] =0`. Simpler not clip? We can leave. But for LSE, negative q_i inside sym? Convolution of nonnegative should be nonnegative; FFT tiny negative ~1e-19. LSE with cm positive, scaled negative. If c has small negative, c/M-1 negative, fine. Don't clip to 0? Not needed. However if c has tiny negative at high indices and cm positive, scaled near -1, e small. fine. In exact max, negative harmless. Let's not clip to avoid altering.
```
out[np.abs(out) < 1e-15] = 0.0? Might be okay but extra. Use `out = np.real(out)`.
```

`compute_score_raw`: Should handle if max negative? max of nonneg. If S<1e-300 inf.
```
def compute_score_raw(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, float)
    if a.size == 0: return np.inf
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
    if M < 0: M = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * M / (S*S))
```

`normalize_a`:
```
def normalize_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(a>0.0, a, 0.0)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
       return np.ones(a.size, float)/a.size
    return a/S
```

`interpolate_a` as above.

`conv_weights_a` as above.

`softmax_from_u`: We may need performance: using np.empty every call. Fine. We can avoid if u length large; okay.
```
def softmax_from_u(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, float).ravel()
    N = int(u.size) + 1
    if N <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return np.ones(N, float) / N
    su = float(np.sum(u))
    if not np.isfinite(su):
        return np.ones(N, float) / N
    theta = np.empty(N, float)
    theta[:-1] = u
    theta[-1] = -su
    m = float(np.max(theta))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(N, float) / N
    w = np.exp(theta - m)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(N, float) / N
    a = w / s
    t = float(np.sum(a))
    if t <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(t):
        return np.ones(N, float) / N
    return a / t
```
`a/t` recomputes sum; okay.

`a_to_u`:
```
def a_to_u(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    N=a.size
    if N <= 1: return np.empty(0,float)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
    th=np.log(a)
    m=float(np.mean(th))
    th = th - m
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
       return np.zeros(N-1,float)
    return th[:-1].copy()
```

def stop_callback(ck):
    return time.monotonic() < _STOP_TIME

`optimize_softmax`:
```
def optimize_softmax(a, target_seconds, beta_schedule=None):
    a=normalize_a(a)
    N=a.size
    if N<=0: return a, np.inf
    best_a=a; best_sc=compute_score_raw(a)
    if target_seconds <= 0: return best_a,best_sc
    if beta_schedule is None:
       if target_seconds < 20:
          beta_schedule=[2000,20000,200000]
       elif target_seconds < 90:
          beta_schedule=[200,2000,20000,200000]
       else:
          beta_schedule=[20,100,1000,10000,50000,250000]
    beta_schedule = [float(b) for b in beta_schedule if b > 0]
    if not beta_schedule:
       beta_schedule=[100.0]
    u = a_to_u(best_a)
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    # measure
    _OBJ_N=N; _OBJ_BETA=1.0
    t0=time.monotonic(); objective_soft(u); objective_soft(u); t_measure=time.monotonic()-t0
    t_obj=max(0.0002, (t_measure/2.0)*1.6)
    # fractions for max len beta schedule
    n_beta=len(beta_schedule)
    fracs=[0.08,0.10,0.14,0.18,0.20,0.18,0.12]
    fr=fracs[:n_beta]
    if n_beta > len(fr):
       fr += [fr[-1]]*(n_beta-len(fr))
    fs=sum(fr)
    for idx,beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
       if time.monotonic() >= start_t + target_seconds - 2: break
       if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5: break
       alloc = target_seconds * (fr[idx] / fs)
       rem_total = start_t + target_seconds - time.monotonic()
       rem_global = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic()
       alloc = min(alloc, rem_total, rem_global)
       if alloc <= 1.0: break
       # cap alloc to avoid a single beta too long? maybe min(alloc, 600)
       maxfun = int(max(150, min(20000, alloc / (t_obj * 1.4))))
       maxiter = int(max(200, maxfun//2))
       _OBJ_N=N; _OBJ_BETA=beta
       stop_t = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, alloc - 0.5)
       if stop_t > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE: stop_t = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 1.0
       _STOP_TIME = stop_t
       try:
          res = minimize(objective_soft, u, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                         options={'maxfun': maxfun, 'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':40, 'maxcor':20, 'disp':False},
                         callback=stop_callback)
       except Exception:
          res=None
       if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          a_new=softmax_from_u(res.x)
          a_new=normalize_a(a_new)
          sc_new=compute_score_raw(a_new)
          if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
             best_sc=float(sc_new); best_a=a_new; u=a_to_u(best_a)
       # Also from current u exact check? not necessary
       # after each beta, print maybe:
       print(f"  opt N={N} beta={beta:.0f} t={time.monotonic()-start_t:.0f}s best={best_sc:.10f}")
    a_final=softmax_from_u(u); a_final=normalize_a(a_final)
    sc_final=compute_score_raw(a_final)
    if sc_final < best_sc:
       best_a=a_final; best_sc=sc_final
    return best_a, best_sc
```
The print per beta may be many but okay. It uses f-string; fine.

Potential: If target_seconds large and fr sum normalizes to 1, alloc each fraction. If a beta terminates early, next beta still uses its fraction, so total target (if all use full) equals target. Good. If a beta uses less, extra time unused because alloc fixed. But rem_total not considered in alloc except min of fixed fraction and rem_total. If beta ended early, rem_total larger, next alloc still fixed fraction; total < target, leaving time. Could use remaining proportional instead: `alloc = min(target_seconds * (fr[idx]/fs), rem_total)`, if early, it still fixed fraction not rem_total. We could define cumulative: `alloc = min(target_seconds*fr[idx]/fs, rem_total)`. If early, rem_total > fixed fraction, so fixed; intended leave slack. Fine.

Could improve by using `alloc = min(target_seconds * (fr[idx]/fs) * 2, rem_total)` to use extra early. But risk overrun. Keep.

The minalloc function `max(150, min(20000, alloc/(t_obj*1.4)))`: If alloc 10 sec, t_obj 0.001 -> 7142, okay. If t_obj 0.01 ->1000. Good.

`_GLOBAL_DEADLINE` initially inf; set in propose. In optimize, if inf, stop_t set to now+alloc. Good.

Implementation exact:
```
def exact_line_polish(a, target_seconds, beta_w_list=None):
    start_t=time.monotonic()
    if target_seconds <= 0: return a, compute_score_raw(a)
    best=np.array(a,float).ravel(); best_sc=compute_score_raw(best); N=best.size
    if N<=1: return best,best_sc
    if beta_w_list is None: beta_w_list=[20.0,100.0,1000.0,5000.0]
    base=max(1e-5, 4.0/float(np.sqrt(N)))
    no_imp=0; it=0; max_it=120
    while time.monotonic() < start_t+target_seconds and it<max_it and no_imp<4:
       c=fast_conv(best)
       if c.size==0: break
       M=float(np.max(c))
       if M <=0: break
       improved=False
       for beta_w in beta_w_list:
          if time.monotonic() >= start_t+target_seconds or time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE: break
          w=peak_weights(c, float(beta_w))
          g=2.0*conv_weights_a(w,best)
          d=-(g - float(np.mean(g)))
          norm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
          if norm < 1e-14: continue
          d=d/norm
          steps=[base*s for s in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0)]
          local_sc=best_sc; local_a=None; local_t=0.0
          for step in steps:
             if time.monotonic() >= start_t+target_seconds or time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE: break
             ap=best + step*d
             if ap.min() < -base: continue
             ap=np.clip(ap,0.0,None)
             Sa=float(np.sum(ap))
             if Sa <= 1e-12: continue
             ap=ap/Sa
             sc=compute_score_raw(ap)
             if sc < local_sc - 1e-15:
                local_sc=sc; local_a=ap; local_t=step
          if local_a is not None:
             # refine
             ref_a=local_a; ref_sc=local_sc
             for fac in (0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5):
                step=local_t*fac
                if step <=0: continue
                ap=best + step*d
                if ap.min() < -base: continue
                ap=np.clip(ap,0.0,None)
                Sa=float(np.sum(ap)); if Sa<=0: continue
                ap=ap/Sa
                sc=compute_score_raw(ap)
                if sc < ref_sc - 1e-15:
                   ref_sc=sc; ref_a=ap
             best=ref_a; best_sc=ref_sc; it+=1; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          no_imp += 1
    return best, best_sc
```
The `if Sa <=0: continue` syntax on one line isn't valid with no colon? Need proper line. We'll format correctly.

`perturb_a` top-level: Use rng. No closures.
```
def perturb_a(a, rng, scale):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); N=a.size
    x=np.linspace(0,1,N)
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
    if mode==0:
       ap=a*np.exp(scale*rng.standard_normal(N))
    elif mode==1:
       freq=1.0+5.0*float(rng.random())
       phase=2.0*np.pi*float(rng.random())
       amp=scale*(1.0+float(rng.random()))
       ap=a*(1.0+amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*freq*x+phase))
    elif mode==2:
       ap=a.copy()
       nb=max(1, N//12)
       bs=max(1, N//nb)
       for i in range(nb):
          s=i*bs; e=min(N,s+bs)
          ap[s:e] *= (1.0 + 4.0*scale*(float(rng.random())-0.5))
    elif mode==3:
       # multiplicative bounded
       ap=a*np.exp(scale*2.0*rng.standard_normal(N))
    else:
       # random subset
       k=max(1, N//4)
       sel=rng.choice(N, size=k, replace=False)
       ap=a.copy()
       ap[sel] *= (1.0 + 3.0*scale*(rng.random(k)-0.5))
    ap=np.where(ap>0, ap, 0.0)
    S=float(np.sum(ap))
    if S<=0: return np.ones(N)/N
    return ap/S
```
`rng.choice` for N large 12000, size 3000 okay. Could be slow but occasionally only. Use mode other.

`coarse_search` top-level. Need use `_GLOBAL_DEADLINE`. It calls optimize which sets _OBJ_N.

```
def coarse_search(parent, rng, target_seconds):
    start_t=time.monotonic()
    if target_seconds <= 5: return []
    if target_seconds > 240:
        n_list=[128,256,512]
    elif target_seconds > 100:
        n_list=[128,256]
    else:
        n_list=[96,160]
    weights=np.array([float(n)**1.1 for n in n_list])
    fractions=weights/float(weights.sum())
    best1=None; sc1=np.inf; best2=None; sc2=np.inf
    for idx,n in enumerate(n_list):
       t_alloc = target_seconds*float(fractions[idx]) - 3.0
       if t_alloc < 15: continue
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       raw=[]
       if parent is not None:
          raw.append(interpolate_a(parent,n))
       raw.append(np.ones(n))
       raw.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.7,n)))
       for B in (-0.75,0.5,1.25):
          raw.append(np.maximum(1.0+B*x,1e-6))
       raw.append(0.2+0.8*x*x)
       raw.append(0.2+0.8*(1.0-x)*(1.0-x))
       scs=[]; arrs=[]
       for r in raw:
          r=normalize_a(r); sc=compute_score_raw(r); scs.append(sc); arrs.append(r)
       order=np.argsort(np.array(scs, dtype=float))
       top=[]
       for i in order:
          if len(top) >= min(4, len(arrs)): break
          top.append(arrs[i])
       local_best=None; local_sc=np.inf
       per_t=t_alloc/max(1,len(top))
       for a0 in top:
          if time.monotonic() > start_t + target_seconds - 5: break
          t_s=min(60.0, per_t*0.55)
          if t_s <= 4: continue
          sc0=compute_score_raw(a0)
          if sc0 < local_sc: local_sc=sc0; local_best=np.array(a0,float)
          a_opt,sc_opt=optimize_softmax(a0, t_s, beta_schedule=[10.0,100.0,1000.0,10000.0])
          if sc_opt < local_sc: local_sc=sc_opt; local_best=a_opt
       if local_best is not None:
          elapsed=time.monotonic()-start_t
          rem=(target_seconds - elapsed)*0.35
          rem=max(0.0, min(150.0, rem))
          if rem > 8:
             a_opt,sc_opt=optimize_softmax(local_best, rem, beta_schedule=[200.0,2000.0,20000.0,200000.0])
             if sc_opt < local_sc: local_sc=sc_opt; local_best=a_opt
          if local_sc < sc1:
             best2,sc2,best1,sc1=best1,sc1,local_best,local_sc
          elif local_sc < sc2:
             best2,sc2=local_best,local_sc
       print(f"coarse n={n} cand_best={local_sc if local_best is not None else np.inf:.10f}")
    out=[]
    if best1 is not None: out.append(best1)
    if best2 is not None: out.append(best2)
    return out
```
Note if local_sc is numpy inf, f-string with `np.inf:.10f` okay? It prints inf; but if local_best None, expression `local_sc if local_best is not None else np.inf`; fine. But formatting np.inf works.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The code uses:
  - a softmax/log-domain re-parameterization (removes sum scaling and bounds),
  - log-sum-exp smoothing of the maximum convolution peak,
  - multi-length and multi-start L-BFGS polishing,
  - a coarse global search to generate diverse good initial shapes,
  - an exact active-weight line-search polish for the last few bits.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_STOP_TIME = 0.0
_OBJ_N = 1
_OBJ_BETA = 1.0
_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = float("inf")


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution via real FFT. Returns length 2n-1."""
    n = int(np.asarray(a).size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
        x = float(a[0])
        return np.array([x * x], dtype=float)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    M = 2 * n - 1
    Mf = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Mf)[:M]
    return np.real(out)


def compute_score_raw(a):
    """Exact score for a numpy array, assuming reasonably non-negative values."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * M / (S * S))


def normalize_a(a):
    """Clip to non-negative and normalize to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=float)
    a = np.where(a > 0.0, a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=float) / float(a.size)
    return a / S


def interpolate_a(a, n):
    """Linearly interpolate a sequence to length n."""
    old = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if old.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(max(1, n))
    if old.size == 1:
        val = float(old[0])
        if not np.isfinite(val) or val <= 0.0:
            val = 1.0
        return np.full(int(n), val, dtype=float)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    out = np.interp(xn, xo, old)
    return out


def conv_weights_a(weights, a):
    """
    Compute 2 is not included here.
    Returns coefficient j of sum_i weights[i] * a[i-j] (with valid range),
    i.e. the base vector entering the gradient for weighted peak sums.
    """
    n = len(a)
    M2 = len(weights) + n - 1
    Mf = 1 << (M2 - 1).bit_length()
    wf = np.fft.rfft(weights, Mf)
    af = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Mf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(wf * af, Mf)[:M2]
    return c[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def softmax_from_u(u):
    """
    Map u (length N-1) to a = softmax(theta), theta = (u, -sum(u)).
    """
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float).ravel()
    N = int(u.size) + 1
    if N <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return np.ones(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    su = float(np.sum(u))
    if not np.isfinite(su):
        return np.ones(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    theta = np.empty(N, dtype=float)
    theta[:-1] = u
    theta[-1] = -su
    m = float(np.max(theta))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    w = np.exp(theta - m)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    a = w / s
    t = float(np.sum(a))
    if t <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(t):
        return np.ones(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    return a / t


def a_to_u(a):
    """Convert a positive-ish vector to the theta difference coordinates u."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    N = int(a.size)
    if N <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    th = np.log(a)
    m = float(np.mean(th))
    th = th - m
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
        return np.zeros(N - 1, dtype=float)
    return th[:-1].copy()


def stop_callback(ck):
    """Scipy minimize callback: stop when wall-clock target is exceeded."""
    return time.monotonic() < _STOP_TIME


def objective_soft(u):
    """
    LSE-smoothed objective and gradient in u-coordinates.
    Global variables: _OBJ_N, _OBJ_BETA.
    """
    N = int(_OBJ_N)
    beta = max(1.0, float(_OBJ_BETA))
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float).ravel()
    a = softmax_from_u(u)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(N - 1, dtype=float)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(N - 1, dtype=float)

    scaled = beta * (c - cm)
    scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0))
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        se = 1.0
    else:
        p = e / se

    lse = cm + (float(np.log(se)) / beta)
    score = float(2.0 * N * lse)

    dL = 2.0 * conv_weights_a(p, a)
    g_a = 2.0 * N * dL
    gs = float(np.sum(g_a))
    gtheta = g_a - a * gs
    grad_u = gtheta[:-1] - gtheta[-1]

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_u)):
        grad_u = np.zeros(N - 1, dtype=float)
    if not np.isfinite(score) or score < 0.0:
        score = 1e100
    return float(score), grad_u


def optimize_softmax(a, target_seconds, beta_schedule=None):
    """
    Polish a nonnegative sequence a using softmax L-BFGS on LSE-smoothed peaks.
    Returns (best_a, best_exact_score).
    """
    a = normalize_a(a)
    N = int(a.size)
    if N <= 0:
        return a, np.inf

    best_a = a.astype(float).copy()
    best_sc = compute_score_raw(best_a)
    if target_seconds <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    if beta_schedule is None:
        if target_seconds < 20.0:
            beta_schedule = [2000.0, 20000.0, 200000.0]
        elif target_seconds < 90.0:
            beta_schedule = [200.0, 2000.0, 20000.0, 200000.0]
        else:
            beta_schedule = [20.0, 100.0, 1000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0, 250000.0]

    beta_schedule = [float(b) for b in beta_schedule if float(b) > 0.0]
    if not beta_schedule:
        beta_schedule = [100.0]

    u = a_to_u(best_a)
    start_t = time.monotonic()

    _OBJ_N = N
    _OBJ_BETA = 1.0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    objective_soft(u)
    objective_soft(u)
    t_measure = time.monotonic() - t0
    t_obj = max(0.0002, (t_measure / 2.0) * 1.6)

    n_beta = len(beta_schedule)
    fracs = [0.08, 0.10, 0.14, 0.18, 0.20, 0.18, 0.12]
    fr = fracs[:n_beta]
    if n_beta > len(fr):
        fr = fr + [fr[-1]] * (n_beta - len(fr))
    fs = float(sum(fr))

    for idx, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        if time.monotonic() >= start_t + target_seconds - 2.0:
            break
        if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0:
            break

        alloc = target_seconds * (float(fr[idx]) / fs)
        rem_total = start_t + target_seconds - time.monotonic()
        rem_global = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic()
        alloc = max(0.0, min(alloc, rem_total, rem_global))
        if alloc <= 1.0:
            break

        maxfun = int(max(150, min(20000, alloc / (t_obj * 1.4))))
        maxiter = int(max(200, maxfun // 2))

        _OBJ_N = N
        _OBJ_BETA = float(beta)
        stop_t = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, alloc - 0.5)
        if stop_t > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
            stop_t = max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 1.0)
        _STOP_TIME = stop_t

        try:
            res = minimize(
                objective_soft,
                u,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxls": 40,
                    "maxcor": 20,
                    "disp": False,
                },
                callback=stop_callback,
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            xx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).ravel()
            if xx.size == int(N) - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(xx)):
                a_new = normalize_a(softmax_from_u(xx))
                sc_new = compute_score_raw(a_new)
                if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc_new)
                    best_a = a_new
                    u = a_to_u(best_a)

        if target_seconds > 30.0:
            print(
                f"    LSE polish N={N} beta={float(beta):.0f} "
                f"t={time.monotonic()-start_t:.0f}s best={best_sc:.10f}"
            )

    a_final = normalize_a(softmax_from_u(u))
    sc_final = compute_score_raw(a_final)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best_a = a_final
        best_sc = float(sc_final)

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def peak_weights(c, beta_w):
    """Weights on convolution peaks, scaled by c/max(c), for exact polish."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.ones_like(c, dtype=float) / max(1, c.size)
    scaled = float(beta_w) * (c / M - 1.0)
    scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -60.0, 0.0))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def exact_line_polish(a, target_seconds, beta_w_list=None):
    """
    Exact line-search polish along weighted strongest-peak subgradient directions.
    """
    a = normalize_a(a)
    if target_seconds <= 0.0:
        return a, compute_score_raw(a)

    start_t = time.monotonic()
    best = a.astype(float).copy()
    best_sc = compute_score_raw(best)
    N = int(best.size)
    if N <= 1:
        return best, best_sc

    if beta_w_list is None:
        beta_w_list = [20.0, 100.0, 1000.0, 5000.0]

    base = max(1e-5, 4.0 / float(np.sqrt(N)))
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    max_it = 120

    while (
        time.monotonic() < start_t + target_seconds
        and time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE
        and it < max_it
        and no_improve < 4
    ):
        c = fast_conv(best)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        improved = False
        for bw in beta_w_list:
            if time.monotonic() >= start_t + target_seconds:
                break
            if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
                break

            w = peak_weights(c, float(bw))
            g = 2.0 * conv_weights_a(w, best)
            d = -(g - float(np.mean(g)))
            normd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
            if normd < 1e-14:
                continue
            d = d / normd

            steps = [
                base * 0.01,
                base * 0.03,
                base * 0.1,
                base * 0.3,
                base * 1.0,
                base * 3.0,
                base * 10.0,
            ]

            local_best_a = None
            local_best_sc = best_sc
            local_t = 0.0

            for step in steps:
                if time.monotonic() >= start_t + target_seconds:
                    break
                if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
                    break
                ap = best + step * d
                if float(np.min(ap)) < -base:
                    continue
                ap = np.clip(ap, 0.0, None)
                Sa = float(np.sum(ap))
                if Sa <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(Sa):
                    continue
                ap = ap / Sa
                sc = compute_score_raw(ap)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc - 1e-15:
                    local_best_sc = sc
                    local_best_a = ap
                    local_t = step

            if local_best_a is not None:
                ref_a = local_best_a
                ref_sc = local_best_sc
                for fac in (0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5):
                    step = local_t * fac
                    if step <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(step):
                        continue
                    ap = best + step * d
                    if float(np.min(ap)) < -base:
                        continue
                    ap = np.clip(ap, 0.0, None)
                    Sa = float(np.sum(ap))
                    if Sa <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(Sa):
                        continue
                    ap = ap / Sa
                    sc = compute_score_raw(ap)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < ref_sc - 1e-15:
                        ref_a = ap
                        ref_sc = sc

                best = ref_a
                best_sc = float(ref_sc)
                it += 1
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1

    return best, float(best_sc)


def perturb_a(a, rng, scale):
    """Random multiplicative / block / wavelet-style perturbation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    N = int(a.size)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))

    if mode == 0:
        ap = a * np.exp(scale * rng.standard_normal(N))
    elif mode == 1:
        freq = 1.0 + 5.0 * float(rng.random())
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        amp = scale * (1.0 + float(rng.random()))
        ap = a * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))
    elif mode == 2:
        ap = a.copy()
        nb = max(1, N // 12)
        bs = max(1, N // nb)
        for i in range(nb):
            s = i * bs
            e = min(N, s + bs)
            ap[s:e] *= (1.0 + 4.0 * scale * (float(rng.random()) - 0.5))
    elif mode == 3:
        ap = a * np.exp(2.0 * scale * rng.standard_normal(N))
    else:
        k = max(1, N // 4)
        sel = rng.choice(N, size=k, replace=False)
        ap = a.copy()
        ap[sel] *= (1.0 + 3.0 * scale * (rng.random(k) - 0.5))

    ap = np.where(ap > 0.0, ap, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(ap))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.ones(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    return ap / S


def coarse_search(parent, rng, target_seconds):
    """
    Search at small/n medium resolutions to generate rich initial shapes.
    Returns a list of 0-2 best coarse sequences.
    """
    if target_seconds <= 5.0:
        return []

    start_t = time.monotonic()

    if target_seconds > 240.0:
        n_list = [128, 256, 512]
    elif target_seconds > 100.0:
        n_list = [128, 256]
    else:
        n_list = [96, 160]

    weights = np.array([float(n) ** 1.1 for n in n_list], dtype=float)
    fractions = weights / float(weights.sum())

    best1 = None
    sc1 = np.inf
    best2 = None
    sc2 = np.inf

    for idx, n in enumerate(n_list):
        t_alloc = target_seconds * float(fractions[idx]) - 3.0
        if t_alloc < 15.0:
            continue

        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
        raw = []

        if parent is not None:
            raw.append(interpolate_a(parent, n))

        raw.append(np.ones(n, dtype=float))
        raw.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, n)))
        for B in (-0.75, 0.5, 1.25):
            raw.append(np.maximum(1.0 + B * x, 1e-6))
        raw.append(0.2 + 0.8 * x * x)
        raw.append(0.2 + 0.8 * (1.0 - x) * (1.0 - x))

        scs = []
        arrs = []
        for r in raw:
            r = normalize_a(r)
            sc = compute_score_raw(r)
            scs.append(sc)
            arrs.append(r)

        order = np.argsort(np.array(scs, dtype=float))
        top = []
        for i in order:
            if len(top) >= min(4, len(arrs)):
                break
            top.append(arrs[i])

        local_best = None
        local_sc = np.inf
        per_t = t_alloc / max(1, len(top))

        for a0 in top:
            if time.monotonic() > start_t + target_seconds - 5.0:
                break
            if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0:
                break
            t_s = min(60.0, per_t * 0.55)
            if t_s <= 4.0:
                continue

            sc0 = compute_score_raw(a0)
            if sc0 < local_sc:
                local_sc = sc0
                local_best = a0.copy()

            a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_softmax(
                a0, t_s, beta_schedule=[10.0, 100.0, 1000.0, 10000.0]
            )
            if sc_opt < local_sc:
                local_sc = sc_opt
                local_best = a_opt

        if local_best is not None:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start_t
            rem = max(0.0, (target_seconds - elapsed) * 0.35)
            rem = min(150.0, rem)
            if rem > 8.0:
                a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_softmax(
                    local_best,
                    rem,
                    beta_schedule=[200.0, 2000.0, 20000.0, 200000.0],
                )
                if sc_opt < local_sc:
                    local_sc = sc_opt
                    local_best = a_opt

            if local_sc < sc1:
                best2, sc2, best1, sc1 = best1, sc1, local_best, local_sc
            elif local_sc < sc2:
                best2, sc2 = local_best, local_sc

        val = local_sc if local_best is not None else np.inf
        print(f"  coarse n={n} best={val:.10f}")

    out = []
    if best1 is not None:
        out.append(best1)
    if best2 is not None:
        out.append(best2)
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search function. Returns a list of non-negative heights.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = t_start + budget - 20.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if p is not None:
            pnp = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
            pnp = np.nan_to_num(pnp, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            pnp = np.clip(pnp, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if pnp.size >= 16 and float(np.sum(pnp)) > 0.0:
                parent = pnp
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        pa = normalize_a(parent)
        ps = compute_score_raw(pa)
        if np.isfinite(ps):
            best_a = pa
            best_sc = float(ps)
            print(f"Parent length={parent.size}, score={ps:.10f}")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=float) * 0.5
        best_sc = compute_score_raw(best_a)
        print(f"Initial uniform length={best_a.size}, score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Coarse global search for good initial shapes
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if parent is not None:
        T_coarse = 0.10 * max(60.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - t_start)
    else:
        T_coarse = 0.20 * max(60.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - t_start)

    coarse_candidates = []
    if T_coarse > 30.0 and time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 30.0:
        actual_coarse = min(T_coarse, max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
        try:
            coarse_candidates = coarse_search(parent, rng, actual_coarse)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"coarse search failed: {exc}")
            coarse_candidates = []

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Choose lengths for high-resolution polishing
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 32:
        n_parent = int(parent.size)
        n_list = [n_parent]

        n_large = int(round(n_parent * 1.5))
        if n_large < n_parent + 512:
            n_large = n_parent + 1024
        n_large = int(min(12000, max(n_parent + 1, n_large)))
        if n_large != n_parent:
            n_list.append(n_large)

        # If the parent is quite short, add a bigger length.
        if n_parent < 4096:
            n_big = int(min(10000, max(n_parent * 2, 8192)))
            if n_big not in n_list:
                n_list.append(n_big)

        # Cap and deduplicate.
        clean = []
        for n in n_list:
            n = int(min(12000, max(64, n)))
            if n not in clean:
                clean.append(n)
        n_list = clean
    else:
        n_list = [8192, 11264]

    fracs_len = {}
    if len(n_list) == 1:
        fracs_len = [1.0]
    elif len(n_list) == 2:
        fracs_len = [0.55, 0.45]
    else:
        fracs_len = [0.40, 0.35, 0.25]

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # High-resolution multi-start polishing
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    total_remaining = max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic())
    print(f"Lengths to try: {n_list}, remaining ~{total_remaining:.0f}s")

    for li, n in enumerate(n_list):
        if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 30.0:
            break
        time_budget = float(fracs_len[li]) * max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic())
        if time_budget < 30.0:
            break

        length_start = time.monotonic()
        n = int(n)
        print(f"High-res length={n}, budget={time_budget:.0f}s")

        raw = []

        # Current global best, interpolated to this length.
        if best_a is not None and best_a.size > 0:
            if best_a.size == n:
                raw.append(normalize_a(best_a))
            else:
                raw.append(interpolate_a(best_a, n))

        # Parent.
        if parent is not None:
            if parent.size == n:
                raw.append(normalize_a(parent))
            else:
                raw.append(interpolate_a(parent, n))

        # Coarse global candidates.
        for ca in coarse_candidates:
            if ca is not None and ca.size > 0:
                if ca.size == n:
                    raw.append(normalize_a(ca))
                else:
                    raw.append(interpolate_a(ca, n))

        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

        for B in (-0.75, 0.5, 1.25):
            raw.append(np.maximum(1.0 + B * x, 1e-6))
        raw.append(0.2 + 0.8 * x * x)
        raw.append(0.2 + 0.8 * (1.0 - x) * (1.0 - x))
        raw.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)))

        # A special endpoint-heavy sequence based on truncated (1-z)^(-1/2).
        if n >= 64:
            c = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
            cv = 1.0
            for j in range(1, n):
                cv = cv * ((2.0 * j - 1.0) / (4.0 * j))
                c[j] = cv
            raw.append(c)

        # Score and sort candidates.
        scs = []
        arrs = []
        for r in raw:
            r = normalize_a(r)
            if r.size != n:
                continue
            sc = compute_score_raw(r)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scs.append(float(sc))
                arrs.append(r)

        top_starts = []
        if best_a is not None:
            b0 = interpolate_a(best_a, n) if best_a.size != n else normalize_a(best_a)
            top_starts.append(b0)

        if arrs:
            order = np.argsort(np.array(scs, dtype=float))
            for i in order:
                if len(top_starts) >= 2:
                    break
                top_starts.append(arrs[i])

            # Forced diversity: one bad/random start.
            if len(top_starts) < 4:
                top_starts.append(arrs[order[-1]])

        # Special endpoint-heavy sequence if not already selected.
        if n >= 64 and len(top_starts) < 4:
            c = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
            cv = 1.0
            for j in range(1, n):
                cv = cv * ((2.0 * j - 1.0) / (4.0 * j))
                c[j] = cv
            top_starts.append(normalize_a(c))

        # Limit and allocate time.
        top_starts = top_starts[:4]
        start_fracs = [0.45, 0.25, 0.15, 0.15]

        for si, a0 in enumerate(top_starts):
            if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 30.0:
                break
            t_opt = time_budget * float(start_fracs[si]) - 8.0
            if t_opt < 12.0:
                break

            a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_softmax(a0, t_opt, beta_schedule=None)

            if sc_opt < best_sc:
                best_a = a_opt
                best_sc = float(sc_opt)
                print(f"  NEW BEST N={n} start#{si}: {best_sc:.10f}")

        # Final polish for this length, using the current global best at this length.
        if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 50.0:
            final_for_n = interpolate_a(best_a, n) if best_a.size != n else normalize_a(best_a)

            rem = max(0.0, time.monotonic() - length_start)
            leftover = time_budget - rem
            if leftover <= 0.0:
                leftover = max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 25.0)

            t_pol = min(60.0, max(0.0, leftover * 0.25))
            if t_pol > 10.0:
                a_pol, sc_pol = exact_line_polish(final_for_n, t_pol)
                if sc_pol < best_sc:
                    best_a = a_pol
                    best_sc = float(sc_pol)
                    print(f"  EXACT POLISH N={n}: {best_sc:.10f}")

            t_opt2 = min(120.0, max(0.0, leftover * 0.35))
            if t_opt2 > 15.0 and time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 30.0:
                a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_softmax(
                    final_for_n,
                    t_opt2,
                    beta_schedule=[5000.0, 50000.0, 250000.0],
                )
                if sc_opt < best_sc:
                    best_a = a_opt
                    best_sc = float(sc_opt)
                    print(f"  FINAL MSE POLISH N={n}: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Last-chance polish on the very best sequence
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 25.0:
        t_rem = min(120.0, max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 25.0))
        if t_rem > 8.0:
            a_pol, sc_pol = exact_line_polish(best_a, t_rem)
            if sc_pol < best_sc:
                best_a = a_pol
                best_sc = float(sc_pol)
                print(f"  GLOBAL EXACT POLISH: {best_sc:.10f}")

        t_rem = min(180.0, max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 25.0))
        if t_rem > 20.0:
            a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_softmax(
                best_a,
                t_rem,
                beta_schedule=[10000.0, 50000.0, 250000.0],
            )
            if sc_opt < best_sc:
                best_a = a_opt
                best_sc = float(sc_opt)
                print(f"  GLOBAL FINAL LSE: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Prepare and verify final list
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_a is None or best_a.size == 0:
        final = np.ones(64, dtype=float) * 0.5
    else:
        final = normalize_a(best_a)
        final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = float(np.sum(final))
        if S < 0.01:
            final = np.ones(64, dtype=float) * 0.5
        else:
            final = final / S

    out = [float(v) for v in final]

    verified = np.inf
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(verified):
        # Conservative fallback.
        if parent is not None:
            final = normalize_a(parent)
            final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
            S = float(np.sum(final))
            if S >= 0.01:
                final = final / S
                out = [float(v) for v in final]
            else:
                out = [0.5] * 64
                final = np.ones(64, dtype=float) * 0.5
        else:
            final = np.ones(64, dtype=float) * 0.5
            out = [float(v) for v in final]

        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = np.inf

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, len={len(out)}")
    print(f"elapsed={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s / {budget:.0f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    return out
```
